# amps-effects imported catalogue — price sources (2026-10-22)

Our price = average of the matched listings for the exact product at retailers worldwide, each converted to AUD inc GST, minus 5% (rounded down to the dollar). Listings more than 60% above or 30% below the median were excluded. Where the product was found at only one retailer (the source retailer of the supplied folder), its Australian GST-inclusive price minus 5% is used and the row is marked "(single listing)".
Conversion: 1 USD = 1.443 AUD, 1 GBP = 1.907 AUD; overseas prices have local tax removed and 10% GST added.
